01:52:46 --> 01:52:52

01:52:48 --> 01:52:53

01:52:51 --> 01:52:55
we always discount the future compared

01:52:52 --> 01:52:57

01:52:54 --> 01:52:58

01:52:56 --> 01:53:01

01:53:01 --> 01:53:07

01:53:03 --> 01:53:09

01:53:06 --> 01:53:11
there's always a discount on the future

01:53:08 --> 01:53:13
and that discount is called time

01:53:10 --> 01:53:14
preference the degree to which we prefer

01:53:12 --> 01:53:16
the present to the future is called our

01:53:13 --> 01:53:18

so the higher uh

01:53:17 --> 01:53:22

01:53:19 --> 01:53:24
about the future and the process of

01:53:21 --> 01:53:26
civilization is the process of lowering

01:53:23 --> 01:53:28
our time preference where we start

01:53:25 --> 01:53:30
caring more for the future we start

01:53:27 --> 01:53:32

01:53:29 --> 01:53:34
